1	import type { ScoeGlobalConfig, ScoeCommandConfig, HealthStatus, LinkTestResult } from './types';
2	
3	// --- State ---
4	
5	export interface CommandIngressState {
6	  readonly commandReceiveCount: number;
7	  readonly commandSuccessCount: number;
8	  readonly commandErrorCount: number;
9	
10	  readonly loadedSatelliteId: string;
11	  readonly scoeFramesLoaded: boolean;
12	  readonly healthStatus: HealthStatus;
13	  readonly linkTestResult: LinkTestResult;
14	  readonly lastCommandCode: string;
15	  readonly receiveCommandSuccess: boolean;
16	
17	  readonly runtimeSeconds: number;
18	  readonly satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: number;
19	  readonly lastErrorReason: string;
20	
21	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig;
22	  readonly activeCommandConfigs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[];
23	}
24	
25	// --- Reader ---
26	
27	export interface CommandIngressStateReader {
28	  getSnapshot(): CommandIngressState;
29	  globalConfig(): ScoeGlobalConfig;
30	  readonly loadedSatelliteId: string;
31	  readonly scoeFramesLoaded: boolean;
32	}
33	
34	// --- Writer ---
35	
36	export interface CommandIngressStateWriter {
37	  setLoaded(satelliteId: string, configs: readonly ScoeCommandConfig[]): void;
38	  resetRuntimeState(): void;
39	  updateStatus(patch: Partial<Omit<CommandIngressState, 'globalConfig' | 'activeCommandConfigs'>>): void;
40	  incrementReceiveCount(): void;
41	  incrementSuccessCount(): void;
42	  incrementErrorCount(reason: string): void;
43	  tickSecond(): void;
44	}
45	
46	// --- Factory ---
47	
48	export interface CommandIngressStateContainer {
49	  readonly reader: CommandIngressStateReader;
50	  readonly writer: CommandIngressStateWriter;
51	}
52	
53	export function createCommandIngressState(
54	  globalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig,
55	): CommandIngressStateContainer {
56	  let state: CommandIngressState = {
57	    commandReceiveCount: 0,
58	    commandSuccessCount: 0,
59	    commandErrorCount: 0,
60	    loadedSatelliteId: '',
61	    scoeFramesLoaded: false,
62	    healthStatus: 'unknown',
63	    linkTestResult: 'unknown',
64	    lastCommandCode: '',
65	    receiveCommandSuccess: false,
66	    runtimeSeconds: 0,
67	    satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,
68	    lastErrorReason: '',
69	    globalConfig,
70	    activeCommandConfigs: [],
71	  };
72	
73	  const reader: CommandIngressStateReader = {
74	    getSnapshot() {
75	      return structuredClone(state);
76	    },
77	    globalConfig() {
78	      return state.globalConfig;
79	    },
80	    get loadedSatelliteId() {
81	      return state.loadedSatelliteId;
82	    },
83	    get scoeFramesLoaded() {
84	      return state.scoeFramesLoaded;
85	    },
86	  };
87	
88	  const writer: CommandIngressStateWriter = {
89	    setLoaded(satelliteId, configs) {
90	      state = {
91	        ...state,
92	        loadedSatelliteId: satelliteId,
93	        scoeFramesLoaded: true,
94	        satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,
95	        activeCommandConfigs: [...configs],
96	      };
97	    },
98	
99	    resetRuntimeState() {
100	      state = {
101	        ...state,
102	        scoeFramesLoaded: false,
103	        loadedSatelliteId: '',
104	        satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: 0,
105	        commandReceiveCount: 0,
106	        commandSuccessCount: 0,
107	        commandErrorCount: 0,
108	        healthStatus: 'unknown',
109	        linkTestResult: 'unknown',
110	        receiveCommandSuccess: false,
111	        lastCommandCode: '',
112	        lastErrorReason: '',
113	        activeCommandConfigs: [],
114	      };
115	    },
116	
117	    updateStatus(patch) {
118	      state = { ...state, ...patch };
119	    },
120	
121	    incrementReceiveCount() {
122	      state = {
123	        ...state,
124	        commandReceiveCount: state.commandReceiveCount + 1,
125	      };
126	    },
127	
128	    incrementSuccessCount() {
129	      state = {
130	        ...state,
131	        commandSuccessCount: state.commandSuccessCount + 1,
132	        receiveCommandSuccess: true,
133	      };
134	    },
135	
136	    incrementErrorCount(reason) {
137	      state = {
138	        ...state,
139	        commandErrorCount: state.commandErrorCount + 1,
140	        lastErrorReason: reason,
141	        receiveCommandSuccess: false,
142	      };
143	    },
144	
145	    tickSecond() {
146	      let next = {
147	        ...state,
148	        runtimeSeconds: state.runtimeSeconds + 1,
149	        receiveCommandSuccess: false,
150	      };
151	
152	      if (!next.scoeFramesLoaded) {
153	        next = {
154	          ...next,
155	          commandReceiveCount: 0,
156	          commandSuccessCount: 0,
157	          commandErrorCount: 0,
158	          healthStatus: 'unknown',
159	          linkTestResult: 'unknown',
160	        };
161	      } else {
162	        next = {
163	          ...next,
164	          satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: next.satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds + 1,
165	        };
166	      }
167	
168	      state = next;
169	    },
170	  };
171	
172	  return { reader, writer };
173	}
174

43	
44	export interface CreateTaskServiceOptions {
45	  readonly sendService: SendServiceProvider;
46	  readonly receiveEventSource: ReceiveEventSource;
47	  readonly timerService?: unknown; // placeholder for future TimerService injection
48	  readonly fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
49	  readonly state?: TaskStateContainer;
50	  readonly now?: () => string;
51	}
52	
53	// --- Factory ---
54	
55	let nextInstanceId = 1;
56	
57	export function createTaskService(options: CreateTaskServiceOptions): TaskService {
58	  const sendService = options.sendService;
59	  const receiveSource = options.receiveEventSource;
60	  const state = options.state ?? createTaskState();
61	  const now = options.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
62	  const fieldValueProvider = options.fieldValueProvider;
63	
64	  // Execution control
65	  const abortResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();
66	  const settleResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();
67	
68	  // Condition matching
69	  const conditionRegistry = new ConditionRegistry();
70	  let receiveSubscription: (() => void) | null = null;
71	  let subscriptionRefCount = 0;
72	
73	  // Wire sub-modules
74	  const lifecycle = createLifecycleManager({
75	    state,
76	    conditionRegistry,
77	    abortResolvers,
78	    settleResolvers,
79	    now,
80	  });
81	
82	  const stepExecutors = createStepExecutors({
83	    state,
84	    sendService,
85	    conditionRegistry,
86	    fieldValueProvider,
87	    now,
88	  });
89	
90	  const errorPolicy = createErrorPolicyHandler({
91	    state,
92	    conditionRegistry,
93	    updateLifecycle: lifecycle.updateLifecycle,
94	    stepExecutors,
95	    now,
96	  });
97	
98	  const loops = createIterationLoops({
99	    state,
100	    conditionRegistry,
101	    updateLifecycle: lifecycle.updateLifecycle,
102	    stepExecutors,
103	    errorPolicy,
104	    fieldValueProvider,
105	    now,
106	  });
107	
108	  // --- Subscription management ---
109	
110	  function ensureSubscription(): void {
111	    if (receiveSubscription) {
112	      subscriptionRefCount++;
113	      return;
114	    }
115	    receiveSubscription = receiveSource.subscribe((input) => {
116	      conditionRegistry.processInput(input);
117	    });
118	    subscriptionRefCount = 1;
119	  }
120	
121	  function releaseSubscription(): void {
122	    subscriptionRefCount--;
123	    if (subscriptionRefCount <= 0 && receiveSubscription) {
124	      receiveSubscription();
125	      receiveSubscription = null;
126	      subscriptionRefCount = 0;
127	    }
128	  }
129	
130	  // --- Execution loop orchestration ---
131	
132	  async function runExecutionLoop(
133	    instanceId: string,
134	    signal: Promise<void>,
135	  ): Promise<void> {
136	    const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
137	    if (!inst) return;
138	
139	    try {
140	      ensureSubscription();
141	
142	      // Use the unified runTask via the loops context
143	      await loops.runTimedLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);
144	
145	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
146	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
147	        lifecycle.completeTask(instanceId);
148	      }
149	    } catch (err) {
150	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
151	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
152	        lifecycle.failTask(instanceId, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
153	      }
154	    } finally {
155	      releaseSubscription();
156	      abortResolvers.delete(instanceId);
157	    }
158	  }
159	
160	  // --- Public API ---
161	
162	  return {
163	    getSnapshot() {
164	      return selectTaskSnapshot(state.getSnapshot());
165	    },
166	
167	    getInstance(instanceId) {
168	      return selectTaskInstance(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);
169	    },
170	
171	    getProgress(instanceId) {
172	      return selectTaskProgress(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);
173	    },
174	
175	    getStatistics() {
176	      return selectTaskStatistics(state.getSnapshot());
177	    },
178	
179	    createTask(definition) {
180	      const instanceId = `task-inst-${nextInstanceId++}`;
181	      return state.createInstance(instanceId, definition);
182	    },
183	
184	    startTask(instanceId) {
185	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
186	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'created') return;
187	
188	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'start');
189	
190	      const signal = lifecycle.createAbortSignal(instanceId);
191	
192	      // Fire-and-forget execution loop
193	      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal);
194	    },
195	
196	    pauseTask(instanceId) {
197	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
198	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return;
199	
200	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'pause');
201	      conditionRegistry.clear();
202	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
203	    },
204	
205	    resumeTask(instanceId) {
206	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
207	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;
208	
209	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'resume');
210	
211	      const signal = lifecycle.createAbortSignal(instanceId);
212	      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal);
213	    },
214	
215	    stopTask(instanceId) {
216	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
217	      if (!inst) return;
218	      if (inst.lifecycle !== 'running' && inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;
219	
220	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'stop');
221	      conditionRegistry.clear();
222	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
223	      lifecycle.resolveSettle(instanceId);
224	    },
225	
226	    stopAll(): number {
227	      const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
228	      let stoppedCount = 0;
229	      for (const inst of snapshot.instances) {
230	        if (inst.lifecycle === 'running' || inst.lifecycle === 'paused') {
231	          lifecycle.updateLifecycle(inst.instanceId, 'stop');
232	          conditionRegistry.clear();
233	          lifecycle.abortInstance(inst.instanceId);
234	          lifecycle.resolveSettle(inst.instanceId);
235	          stoppedCount++;
236	        }
237	      }
238	      return stoppedCount;
239	    },
240	
241	    removeTask(instanceId) {
242	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
243	      if (!inst) return;
244	      if (!isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;
245	
246	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
247	      state.removeInstance(instanceId);
248	    },
249	
250	    retryTask(sourceInstanceId) {
251	      const source = state.getInstance(sourceInstanceId);
252	      if (!source || !isTerminal(source.lifecycle)) return undefined;
253	
254	      const newInstanceId = `task-inst-${nextInstanceId++}`;
255	      state.createInstance(newInstanceId, source.definitionRef);
256	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(newInstanceId, 'start');
257	      const signal = lifecycle.createAbortSignal(newInstanceId);
258	      runExecutionLoop(newInstanceId, signal);
259	      return state.getInstance(newInstanceId);
260	    },
261	
262	    updateTask(instanceId, definition) {
263	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
264	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'created') return undefined;
265	      return state.updateInstance(instanceId, { definitionRef: definition });
266	    },
267	
268	    async onSettled(instanceId) {
269	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
270	      if (!inst || isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;
271	
272	      await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
273	        settleResolvers.set(instanceId, resolve);
274	      });
275	    },
276	  };
277	}
278	
```

1	import type { ConditionTerm, ConditionMatchInput } from '../core';
2	import { evaluateConditionGroup } from '../core';
3	
4	interface RegistryGroup {
5	  readonly id: string;
6	  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[];
7	  readonly onSatisfied: () => void;
8	}
9	
10	export interface ConditionGroup {
11	  readonly _group: RegistryGroup;
12	}
13	
14	export class ConditionRegistry {
15	  private groups: RegistryGroup[] = [];
16	  private frameIndex = new Map<string, Set<number>>();
17	  private nextGroupId = 1;
18	
19	  registerGroup(conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[], onSatisfied: () => void): ConditionGroup {
20	    const id = `cg-${this.nextGroupId++}`;
21	    const group: RegistryGroup = { id, conditions, onSatisfied };
22	    const idx = this.groups.length;
23	    this.groups.push(group);
24	    this.indexGroup(idx, group);
25	    return { _group: group };
26	  }
27	
28	  unregisterGroup(group: ConditionGroup): void {
29	    const idx = this.groups.indexOf(group._group);
30	    if (idx === -1) return;
31	    this.groups.splice(idx, 1);
32	    this.rebuildIndex();
33	  }
34	
35	  processInput(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {
36	    const candidateIndices = this.frameIndex.get(input.frameId);
37	    if (!candidateIndices) return;
38	
39	    for (const idx of candidateIndices) {
40	      const group = this.groups[idx];
41	      if (!group) continue;
42	
43	      // sourceId filter
44	      if (input.sourceId !== undefined) {
45	        const hasSourceMatch = group.conditions.some(
46	          (c) => c.sourceId === undefined || c.sourceId === input.sourceId,
47	        );
48	        if (!hasSourceMatch) continue;
49	      }
50	
51	      if (evaluateConditionGroup(group.conditions, { ...input.fieldValues })) {
52	        group.onSatisfied();
53	      }
54	    }
55	  }
56	
57	  clear(): void {
58	    this.groups = [];
59	    this.frameIndex.clear();
60	  }
61	
62	  get size(): number {
63	    return this.groups.length;
64	  }
65	
66	  private indexGroup(idx: number, group: RegistryGroup): void {
67	    for (const cond of group.conditions) {
68	      if (!this.frameIndex.has(cond.frameId)) {
69	        this.frameIndex.set(cond.frameId, new Set());
70	      }
71	      this.frameIndex.get(cond.frameId)!.add(idx);
72	    }
73	  }
74	
75	  private rebuildIndex(): void {
76	    this.frameIndex.clear();
77	    for (let i = 0; i < this.groups.length; i++) {
78	      this.indexGroup(i, this.groups[i]!);
79	    }
80	  }
81	}
82

```
1	import type { ConditionTerm } from './types';
2	import { compareValues } from '@/shared/condition-operators';
3	
4	export function evaluateSingleCondition(
5	  condition: ConditionTerm,
6	  fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null>,
7	): boolean {
8	  const value = fieldValues[condition.fieldId];
9	  if (value === null || value === undefined) return false;
10	  return compareValues(value, condition.threshold, condition.operator);
11	}
12	
13	export function evaluateConditionGroup(
14	  conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[],
15	  fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null>,
16	): boolean {
17	  if (conditions.length === 0) return true;
18	
19	  let result = evaluateSingleCondition(conditions[0]!, fieldValues);
20	
21	  for (let i = 1; i < conditions.length; i++) {
22	    const matches = evaluateSingleCondition(conditions[i]!, fieldValues);
23	    const logicOp = conditions[i]!.logicOperator ?? 'and';
24	
25	    if (logicOp === 'or') {
26	      result = result || matches;
27	      if (result) break; // OR short-circuit
28	    } else {
29	      result = result && matches;
30	      if (!result) break; // AND short-circuit
31	    }
32	  }
33	  return result;
34	}
35

```
1	import type { ComparisonOperator } from './types';
2	
3	export function compareValues(actual: unknown, threshold: unknown, operator: ComparisonOperator): boolean {
4	  if (operator === 'any') {
5	    return actual != null;
6	  }
7	
8	  if (operator === 'change') {
9	    return actual != null && threshold != null && actual !== threshold;
10	  }
11	
12	  if (operator === 'contains') {
13	    if (actual == null || threshold == null) return false;
14	    return String(actual).includes(String(threshold));
15	  }
16	
17	  if (actual == null || threshold == null) {
18	    return false;
19	  }
20	
21	  const actualNum = toNumber(actual);
22	  const thresholdNum = toNumber(threshold);
23	
24	  const useNumeric = actualNum != null && thresholdNum != null;
25	
26	  if (useNumeric) {
27	    return numericCompare(actualNum, thresholdNum, operator);
28	  }
29	
30	  return stringCompare(String(actual), String(threshold), operator);
31	}
32	
33	function toNumber(value: unknown): number | null {
34	  if (typeof value === 'number') {
35	    return Number.isNaN(value) ? null : value;
36	  }
37	  if (typeof value === 'string') {
38	    if (value.startsWith('0x') || value.startsWith('0X')) {
39	      const parsed = Number.parseInt(value, 16);
40	      return Number.isNaN(parsed) ? null : parsed;
41	    }
42	    const parsed = Number(value);
43	    return Number.isNaN(parsed) ? null : parsed;
44	  }
45	  if (typeof value === 'bigint') {
46	    return Number(value);
47	  }
48	  if (typeof value === 'boolean') {
49	    return value ? 1 : 0;
50	  }
51	  return null;
52	}
53	
54	function numericCompare(a: number, b: number, op: ComparisonOperator): boolean {
55	  switch (op) {
56	    case 'eq': return a === b;
57	    case 'neq': return a !== b;
58	    case 'gt': return a > b;
59	    case 'lt': return a < b;
60	    case 'gte': return a >= b;
61	    case 'lte': return a <= b;
62	    default: return false;
63	  }
64	}
65	
66	function stringCompare(a: string, b: string, op: ComparisonOperator): boolean {
67	  switch (op) {
68	    case 'eq': return a === b;
69	    case 'neq': return a !== b;
70	    case 'gt': return a > b;
71	    case 'lt': return a < b;
72	    case 'gte': return a >= b;
73	    case 'lte': return a <= b;
74	    default: return false;
75	  }
76	}
77

{
  "description": "T009+T010 expression-receive-send tests",
  "mode": "bypassPermissions",
  "prompt": "## Task\n\nWrite 2 integration test files in `rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/`:\n\n1. `expression-receive-integration.spec.ts` (T009)\n2. `expression-send-integration.spec.ts` (T010)\n\n## T009: Expression engine → receive integration\n\nTest the full receive processor pipeline with real expression compilation and evaluation. Key code locations:\n\n- Expression compilation: `rewrite/src/features/receive/core/expression-pass.ts` → `compileFrameExpressions(frames)` and `evaluateFrameExpressions(input)`\n- Processor: `rewrite/src/features/receive/core/processor.ts` → `processReceiveBatch(input)` which calls expression-pass when `input.expressionCache` is provided\n- Types: `ExpressionCompileCache = ReadonlyMap<string, FrameExpressionCompileResult>` from `rewrite/src/features/receive/core/types.ts`\n\nTest cases needed:\n1. **Dependency ordering (Kahn's algorithm)**: Create a frame with 3 fields where fieldC depends on fieldB which depends on fieldA. Verify all computed correctly in order.\n2. **Circular dependency detection**: Create a frame where fieldA depends on fieldB and fieldB depends on fieldA. Verify compile returns `{ success: false, error containing 'circular' }`.\n3. **Partial expression failure isolation**: Create a frame with 3 expression fields where the middle one has an invalid expression. Verify the other 2 still evaluate correctly.\n4. **globalParams as empty Map**: When `globalParams` is `new Map()`, expressions using global_stat variables should not crash—they should fail gracefully (error on that field).\n5. **Full processor pipeline with expressions**: Create a `FrameAsset` with expression fields, compile the cache, construct a `ReceiveProcessInput` with matching bytes, call `processReceiveBatch`, and verify the outcome has `expressionApplied: true` on expression fields with correct computed values.\n\nTo construct test frames, use the `FrameAsset` type from `@/features/frame`. Key frame shape:\n```ts\ninterface FrameAsset {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  direction: 'receive' | 'send' | 'both';\n  fields: FrameFieldAsset[];\n  options?: { bigEndian?: boolean };\n}\ninterface FrameFieldAsset {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  dataType: 'uint8' | 'uint16' | 'uint32' | 'int8' | 'int16' | 'int32' | 'float32' | 'float64' | 'bytes' | 'string';\n  length: number;\n  offset: number;\n  factor?: number;\n  expressionConfig?: {\n    expressions: { condition: string; expression: string }[];\n    variables: ExpressionVariableDefinition[];\n  };\n  // ... other optional fields\n}\n```\n\nFor `ExpressionVariableDefinition`:\n```ts\ntype ExpressionVariableDefinition = {\n  identifier: string;\n  sourceType: 'current_field' | 'frame_field' | 'global_stat';\n  fieldId?: string;\n  frameId?: string;\n};\n```\n\nFor `processReceiveBatch`, the `ReceiveProcessInput` needs:\n```ts\ninterface ReceiveProcessInput {\n  batch: ReceiveInputBatch;\n  reference: { frames: FrameReferenceSnapshot[] };\n  processedAt: string;\n  expressionCache?: ExpressionCompileCache;\n}\n```\n\nFor `ReceiveInputBatch`:\n```ts\ninterface ReceiveInputBatch {\n  id: string;\n  bytes: number[];\n  source: { sourceId: string; connectionId: string; kind: string; label: string };\n}\n```\n\nFor `FrameReferenceSnapshot`, import `cloneFrameReference` from receive core to understand what fields are needed. The frame reference needs at minimum: `{ id, name, direction, identifierRules, fields: [{ id, name, dataType, length, offset, factor }] }`.\n\nTo match a frame, the receive processor uses `matchReceiveFrame(bytes, frames)`. The frame needs identifier rules. Simplest approach: use a frame with an identifier rule like `{ kind: 'fixed-bytes', offset: 0, bytes: [0xAA, 0xBB] }` so the first 2 bytes of input must match.\n\n## T010: Expression engine → send integration\n\nTest the send frame resolver with expressions. Key code:\n\n- `rewrite/src/features/send/core/frame-resolver.ts` → `resolveFieldValues()`, `evaluateFieldExpressions()`, `applyFactor()`\n- `frameToBuildInput(frame)` converts a `FrameAsset` to `SendFieldEncodingDef[]`\n\nTest cases needed:\n1. **compileConditional + evaluateConditional in send context**: Create a `SendFieldEncodingDef` with `expressionConfig`, call `evaluateFieldExpressions`, verify result value.\n2. **Factor inverse transform (factor != 1)**: Create fields with factor=0.1, verify `applyFactor` divides by factor.\n3. **defaultValue fallback**: When no expression config and no user value, verify `defaultValue` is used.\n4. **Conditional expression branch selection**: Create expression with condition `{ condition: \"x > 5\", expression: \"x * 2\" }` and `{ condition: \"1\", expression: \"x + 1\" }`, verify correct branch selected.\n5. **Full resolveFieldValues pipeline**: Create multiple fields (configurable, expression, default), call `resolveFieldValues`, verify all resolved correctly with correct priority order.\n\nFor `SendFieldEncodingDef`:\n```ts\ninterface SendFieldEncodingDef {\n  id: string;\n  dataType: string;\n  length: number;\n  bigEndian: boolean;\n  isASCII: boolean;\n  offset: number;\n  factor: number;\n  defaultValue?: string;\n  configurable?: boolean;\n  expressionConfig?: { expressions: { condition: string; expression: string }[]; variables: ExpressionVariableDefinition[] };\n  validOption?: unknown;\n}\n```\n\nFor `SendVariableProvider`:\n```ts\ninterface SendVariableProvider {\n  getVariables(): Map<string, number | string | boolean>;\n}\n```\n\n## Rules\n\n- Use only Vitest + Node built-in modules, no new dependencies\n- Test files go in `rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/`\n- Import from `@/` path aliases (configured in vitest)\n- Do NOT copy production code into test files\n- Do NOT use setTimeout/Promise tricks to test call order\n- If you find a fixable bug during testing, fix it in production code and update test expectations\n- Each test file should be self-contained with its own helpers/fixtures\n- Follow the pattern from existing tests in `rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/bootstrap-integrity.spec.ts`\n"
}

Key code:\n\n- `rewrite/src/features/task/core/condition-matcher.ts` → `evaluateConditionGroup(conditions, fieldValues)` and `evaluateSingleCondition(condition, fieldValues)`\n- `rewrite/src/features/task/services/condition-registry.ts` → `ConditionRegistry` class with `registerGroup()`, `processInput()`, `clear()`\n- `rewrite/src/shared/condition-operators/compare.ts` → `compareValues(actual, threshold, operator)`\n- `rewrite/src/features/task/core/types.ts` → `ConditionTerm { frameId, fieldId, operator, threshold, logicOperator?, sourceId? }`\n\nTest cases needed:\n1. **evaluateConditionGroup AND combination**: Two conditions both must match. First true + second true → true. First true + second false → false (short-circuit).\n2. **evaluateConditionGroup OR combination**: First true → true (short-circuit). Both false → false.\n3. **Mixed AND/OR**: 3 conditions: first AND second OR third. Verify correct evaluation.\n4. **ConditionRegistry with processInput**: Register a condition group for frameId='f1', fieldId='temp', operator='gt', threshold=50. Call `processInput({ frameId: 'f1', fieldValues: { temp: 60 } })`. Verify onSatisfied callback fires.\n5. **ConditionRegistry sourceId filter**: Register condition with sourceId='src-A'. Send input with sourceId='src-B' → no match. Send input with sourceId='src-A' → match.\n6. **Multiple condition groups**: Register 2 groups for same frameId. **文件已创建：**`/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/command-ingress-state-machine.spec.ts`

**内容：**13个测试，分布在6个 `describe` 块中，涵盖所有要求的场景：

1.  **阶段1（2个测试）：**当 `scoeFramesLoaded=false` 时，只有 `LOAD_SATELLITE_ID`（代码 `01`）被消费；所有其他 `SCOE` 命令（`UNLOAD`、`HEALTH`、`SEND_FRAME`）都被拒绝并进入 `remaining`。

2.  **阶段2（2个测试）：**在 `writer.setLoaded()` 之后，所有有效的 `SCOE` 事件都被消费；非 `SCOE` 数据事件仍进入 `remaining`。

3.  **`UNLOAD` 重置为阶段1（1个测试）：**通过 `setLoaded` 到达阶段2后，调用 `resetRuntimeState()` 会将适配器恢复到阶段1行为，即非 `LOAD` 事件再次被拒绝。

4.  **校验和验证（2个测试）：**有效校验和成功解析；损坏的校验和会触发 `onParseError` 回调，并返回正确的错误消息和原始字节，但 `onCommand` 未被调用，且事件仍然被消费（因为 `canHandle` 仅进行字节级检查）。

5.  **`onCommand` 回调（2个测试）：**为每个消费的事件调用回调时，`ParsedCommand` 包含正确的 `commandCode`、`commandFunction`、`commandId`、`rawBytes`、`connectionId` 和 `occurredAt`；当 `onParseError` 触发时，`onCommand` 不会被调用。

6.  **非数据事件（3个测试）：**`connected`、`disconnected`、`error` 种类的事件以及没有 `bytes` 字段的数据事件都进入 `remaining`；在阶段1中混合非数据和非 `SCOE` 数据的事件，只有 `LOAD` 被消费。

7.  **完整生命周期（1个测试）：**阶段1 -> `LOAD` -> 阶段2 -> 重置 -> 阶段1的往返验证。

**集成测试期间发现的关键发现：**当调用 `setLoaded()` 并带有卫星特定命令（例如 `code: '05', checksums: []`）时，`getCommandConfigs()` 会将卫星配置与基本内置命令合并，优先使用卫星版本。这意味着 `send_frame` 的卫星配置（不带校验和）会覆盖基本 `sendFrameCommand`（带校验和）。需要校验和验证的测试使用空的卫星配置列表，以确保使用带有校验和的基本配置。

   - 在没有表达式缓存的情况下进行匹配和解析（不应用任何表达式）

2. `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/expression-send-integration.spec.ts` (T010) -- 13 个测试用例：
   - 通过 `evaluateFieldExpressions` 进行表达式字段评估
   - 当 `factor != 1` 时除以因子（逆变换）
   - 当 `factor=0` 时报告错误
   - `bytes` 类型忽略因子
   - 当没有表达式配置和用户值时，使用 `defaultValue`
   - 字符串默认值保留为字符串
   - 在没有值来源时进行零填充并带有警告
   - 条件表达式分支选择（`x > 5` vs fallback）

   - `frameToBuildInput` 保留表达式配置
   - 表达式语法错误产生编译错误
   - 可配置字段的用户值覆盖表达式

**修复的关键发现：** 表达式条件在评估器中使用严格相等（`condValue === true`），因此条件必须使用 `'true'`（布尔值）而不是 `'1'`（数字）。现有代码库测试也使用了 `condition: 'true'`。

**验证：** 21/21 个测试通过，两个文件的 lint 检查干净。

129	/**
130	 * Build a SCOE-compatible byte array:
131	 *   byte[fcOffset]   = identifier (0x55)
132	 *   byte[fcOffset+1] = commandCode
133	 *   byte[fcOffset+2] = 0xAA
134	 *   byte[fcOffset+3] = 0xAA
135	 *   + optional extra bytes appended after
136	 */
137	function buildScoeBytes(commandCode: number, extra: number[] = []): number[] {
138	  const fcOffset = globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;
139	  const identByte = parseInt(globalConfig.scoeIdentifier.replace(/^0x/i, ''), 16);
140	  const frame: number[] = [];
141	  while (frame.length < fcOffset + 4) frame.push(0);
142	  frame[fcOffset] = identByte;
143	  frame[fcOffset + 1] = commandCode;
144	  frame[fcOffset + 2] = 0xaa;
145	  frame[fcOffset + 3] = 0xaa;
146	  return [...frame, ...extra];
147	}
148	
149	/**
150	 * Build SCOE bytes + append a valid checksum for `sendFrameCommand`.
151	 * Checksum config: offset=4, length=4, checksumOffset=8.
152	 */
153	function buildScoeBytesWithChecksum(commandCode: number): number[] {
154	  const extra = [0x10, 0x20, 0x30, 0x40]; // 4 payload bytes
155	  const frame = buildScoeBytes(commandCode, extra);
156	  // Ensure frame long enough for checksum byte at offset 8
157	  while (frame.length <= 8) frame.push(0);
158	  const cs = sendFrameCommand.checksums[0]!;
159	  let sum = 0;
160	  for (let i = cs.offset; i < cs.offset + cs.length; i++) {
161	    sum += frame[i] ?? 0;
162	  }
163	  frame[cs.checksumOffset] = sum % 256;
164	  return frame;
165	}
166	
167	/** Bytes that do NOT match SCOE identifier. */
168	function buildNonScoeBytes(): number[] {
169	  return [0xde, 0xad, 0xbe, 0xef, 0x00, 0x00];
170	}
171

'05',\n  function: 'send_frame',\n  checksums: [\n    { enabled: true, offset: 4, length: 4, checksumOffset: 8 },\n  ],\n  params: [],\n};\n\nconst baseCommandConfigs: ScoeCommandConfig[] = [\n globalConfig.functionCodeOffset;\n  const identByte = parseInt(globalConfig.scoeIdentifier.replace(/^0x/i, ''), 16);\n  const frame: number[] = [];\n  while (frame.length < fcOffset + 4) frame.push(0);\n  frame[fcOffset] = identByte;\n  frame[fcOffset + 1] = commandCode;\n  frame[fcOffset + 2] = 0xaa;\n  frame[fcOffset + 3] = 0xaa;\n  return [...frame, ...extra];\n}\n\n/**\n * Build SCOE bytes + append a valid checksum for `sendFrameCommand`.\n * Checksum config: offset=4, length=4, checksumOffset=8.\n */\nfunction buildScoeBytesWithChecksum(commandCode: number): number[] {\n  const extra = [0x10, 0x20, 0x30, 0x40]; // 4 payload bytes\n  const frame = buildScoeBytes(commandCode, extra);\n  // Ensure frame long enough for checksum byte at offset 8\n  while (frame.length <= 8) frame.push(0);\n  const cs = sendFrameCommand.checksums[0]!;\n  let sum = 0;\n  for (let i = cs.offset; i < cs.offset + cs.length; i++) {\n    sum += frame[i] ?? 0;\n  }\n  frame[cs.checksumOffset] = sum % 256;\n  return frame;\n}\n\n/** Bytes that do NOT match SCOE identifier. */\nfunction buildNonScoeBytes(): number[] {\n  return [0xde, 0xad, 0xbe, 0xef, 0x00, 0x00];\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Tests\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  resetIds();\n});\n\ndescribe('T014: Command-ingress two-phase state machine', () => {\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 1. // UNLOAD (code 02)\n      const scoeLoadEvent = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x01));    // LOAD (code 01)\n\n      const result = await adapter.consume([nonScoeEvent, scoeNonLoadEvent, scoeLoadEvent]);\n\n      expect(result.consumed).toEqual([scoeLoadEvent]);\n      expect(result.remaining).toEqual([nonScoeEvent, scoeNonLoadEvent]);\n\n      // onCommand called once for the LOAD\n      expect(onCommand).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();\n      const parsed: ParsedCommand = onCommand.mock.calls[0][0];\n      expect(parsed.commandCode).toBe('01');\n      expect(parsed.commandFunction).toBe('load_satellite_id');\n\n      // State unchanged — adapter does not transition state; handler does.\n      expect(reader.scoeFramesLoaded).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it('phase 1 rejects HEALTH_CHECK, SEND_FRAME, and UNLOAD', async () => {\n      const { reader } = createCommandIngressState(globalConfig);\n      const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n        globalConfig,\n        commandConfigs: baseCommandConfigs,\n        stateReader: reader,\n      });\n\n      const healthEvent = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x03));\n      const sendEvent = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x05));\n      const unloadEvent = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x02));\n\n      const result = await adapter.consume([healthEvent, sendEvent, unloadEvent]);\n      expect(result.consumed).toHaveLength(0);\n      expect(result.remaining).toHaveLength(3);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 2. Phase 2: all SCOE commands available\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  describe('phase 2 — all SCOE commands are consumed', () => {\n    it('after setLoaded, all valid SCOE events are consumed', async () => {\n      const { reader, writer } = createCommandIngressState(globalConfig);\n      writer.setLoaded('SAT-99', satelliteCommands);\n      expect(reader.scoeFramesLoaded).toBe(true);\n\n      const onCommand = vi.fn();\n      const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n        globalConfig,\n        commandConfigs: baseCommandConfigs,\n        stateReader: reader,\n        onCommand,\n      });\n\n      const loadEvent = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x01));\n      const unloadEvent = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x02));\n      const healthEvent = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x03));\n      const sendEvent = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytesWithChecksum(0x05));\n\n      const result = await adapter.consume([loadEvent, unloadEvent, healthEvent, sendEvent]);\n\n      expect(result.consumed).toEqual([loadEvent, unloadEvent, healthEvent, sendEvent]);\n      expect(result.remaining).toEqual([]);\n\n 'load_satellite_id',\n        'unload_satellite_id',\n        'health_check',\n        'send_frame',\n      ]);\n    });\n\n    it('non-SCOE data events still go to remaining in phase 2', async () => {\n      const { reader, writer } = createCommandIngressState(globalConfig);\n      writer.setLoaded('SAT-99', satelliteCommands);\n\n      const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n        globalConfig,\n        commandConfigs: baseCommandConfigs,\n        stateReader: reader,\n      });\n\n      const scoeEvent = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x01));\n      const nonScoe1 = makeDataEvent(buildNonScoeBytes());\n      const nonScoe2 = makeDataEvent([0x10, 0x20, 0x30, 0x40, 0x50, 0x60]);\n\n      const result = await adapter.consume([scoeEvent, nonScoe1, nonScoe2]);\n      expect(result.consumed).toEqual([scoeEvent]);\n      expect(result.remaining).toEqual([nonScoe1, nonScoe2]);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 3. UNLOAD resets to phase 1\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  describe('resetRuntimeState returns to phase 1', () => {\n    it('after LOAD then reset, non-LOAD SCOE events go to remaining again', async () => {\n      const { reader, writer } = createCommandIngressState(globalConfig);\n\n      // Transition to phase 2\n      writer.setLoaded('SAT-01', satelliteCommands);\n      expect(reader.scoeFramesLoaded).toBe(true);\n\n      const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n        globalConfig,\n        commandConfigs: baseCommandConfigs,\n        stateReader: reader,\n      });\n\n      // Verify phase 2 works\n      const phase2Load = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x01));\n      const phase2Send = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x05));\n      const phase2Result = await adapter.consume([phase2Load, phase2Send]);\n      expect(phase2Result.consumed).toHaveLength(2);\n\n      // Reset to phase 1\n      writer.resetRuntimeState();\n      expect(reader.scoeFramesLoaded).toBe(false);\n      expect(reader.loadedSatelliteId).toBe('');\n\n      // Re-create adapter so it picks up fresh state\n      const adapterPhase1 = createScoeProtocolAdapter({\n        globalConfig,\n        commandConfigs: baseCommandConfigs,\n        stateReader: reader,\n      });\n\n      const nonLoadEvent = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x05));\n      const loadEvent = makeDataEvent(buildScoeBytes(0x01));\n\n      const result = await adapterPhase1.consume([nonLoadEvent, loadEvent]);\n      expect(result.consumed).toEqual([loadEvent]);\n      expect(result.remaining).toEqual([nonLoadEvent]);\n    });\n  });\n\n  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 4. > AGENT

两轮集测写下来，有三条发现足够通用、足够容易踩：

## 1. Expression condition 必须求值为 `true`，不是 truthy

`evaluateConditional` 里是 `condValue === true`。这意味着：

- `condition: '1'` → 数字 1，**不触发**
- `condition: 'x > 0'` → 布尔 true，触发
- `condition: 'true'` → 布尔 true，触发

这个坑同时影响 receive expression-pass 和 send frame-resolver（都调 `evaluateConditional`）。写表达式的人直觉上觉得"非零即真"，但实际必须显式比较或布尔字面量。

**建议加一条 R17 或放进 §8 集测编写规范下面**：

> Expression condition 表达式必须求值为 `boolean true`（`=== true` 严格比较）。禁止裸数字、裸变量作为 condition。所有 expression condition 必须是显式比较表达式（`>`、`<`、`==`、`>=`、`<=`、`!=`）或布尔字面量 `'true'`/`'false'`。

## 2. 配置分层合并不能静默丢失安全约束

SCOE adapter 的 `getCommandConfigs()` 合并卫星配置 + 基础配置时，卫星版同名 command 覆盖基础版。如果卫星 `send_frame` 没配 checksums，基础版带 checksum 的 `send_frame` 就被静默替换了——校验和验证直接消失。

这不是 SCOE 独有的问题，任何"子配置覆盖父配置"的场景都会遇到。

**建议加到 R14 或独立一条**：

> 配置分层合并（卫星覆盖基础、实例覆盖模板）时，子层不得静默丢失父层的安全约束（checksum、validation、范围检查）。如果子层省略了父层存在的约束字段，合并逻辑必须显式继承或显式标记"确认降级"。

## 3. 条件 AND/OR 无优先级，左结合

`evaluateConditionGroup` 对 AND/OR 没有优先级分组，纯粹从左到右。`A AND B OR C` 实际是 `(A AND B) OR C`，但多数人期望的是 `A AND (B OR C)`。

这个行为是设计决策不是 bug，但**写条件的人几乎一定会误用**。

**建议加到 §8 或 task design 文档**：

> `ConditionTerm` 的 AND/OR 组合是左结合、无优先级。多条件混合 AND/OR 时，必须在设计文档和 checklist 中显式标注期望的求值顺序。不允许依赖"直觉优先级"。

---

三条里第一条影响面最大（receive/send 表达式都受影响），第二条是架构级风险，第三条是设计约定。要不要直接写进去？

291	### R16. Rewrite infrastructure must stay minimal and explicit
292	
293	MUST:
294	
295	- 将 `rewrite/` 作为新的独立应用根目录，而不是只把源码挪到一个子目录。

394	### 集成测试编写规范
395	
396	测试必须测真实的东西，不测运行时的内部调度：
397	
398	- **禁止复制生产代码到测试文件**。如果测试需要访问某个未导出的类或函数，优先从生产代码中 `export` 它（可标注 `@internal`），不要在测试文件里抄一份。复制品不会随生产代码同步更新，等于没测。
399	- **禁止用 setTimeout(0)、Promise 竞争、advanceTimersByTime(0) 等手段测试调用顺序**。调用顺序依赖 V8 microtask 队列的内部调度，在 CI 环境不稳定。改为用因果链验证：A 失败时 B 是否仍被调用、A 成功时 B 是否收到正确输入。
400	- **测试中发现的一行可修 bug，当场修掉并更新测试期望**。不要把生产 bug freeze 成 `expect().rejects.toThrow()` 或"known behavior"就放过。修法包括：加 null check、补 await、移除 `!` non-null assertion。修完后测试期望从"会崩"改为"正确处理"。
401	
402	## 9. Quality Gate
403	
404	进入实现前：
405	
406	- 必须确认目标功能域和目录归口。
407	- 必须确认是否涉及 northbound gap 或 SCOE 例外。
408	- 必须确认旧行为 oracle 来源。
409	- 必须确认直接合同和边界护栏。
410	- 必须确认本 feature 的 validation level、fixture/oracle 位置、fake adapter 计划、manual checklist、runtime/hardware/package/customer blocker。

Expression conditions must evaluate to boolean `true`\n\nMUST:\n\n- Expression condition 表达式必须求值为 `boolean true`（`=== true` 严格比较）。\n- 所有 expression condition 必须是显式比较表达式（`>`、`<`、`==`、`>=`、`<=`、`!=`）或布尔字面量 `'true'`/`'false'`。\n\nMUST NOT:\n\n- 裸数字作为 condition（`'1'` 求值为数字 1，不触发分支）。\n- 裸变量作为 condition（`'x'` 求值为变量值，非布尔时不触发）。\n\nWHY: `evaluateConditional` 使用 `condValue === true` 严格比较。`'1'`、`'x'`（x 为正数）等 truthy 值不触发条件分支。此行为同时影响 receive expression-pass 和 send frame-resolver，是表达式消费方最容易犯的错误。\n\n### R18. Config layer merging must not silently drop safety constraints\n\nMUST:\n\n- 配置分层合并（卫星覆盖基础、实例覆盖模板、子 scope 覆盖父 scope）时，子层省略的安全约束字段必须显式继承自父层。\n- 若子层确认需要降级或去掉某个安全约束（如去掉 checksum），必须在配置中显式标记（如 `checksumOverride: 'none'`），不允许靠\"省略字段\"隐式跳过。\n\nMUST NOT:\n\n- 靠子配置省略字段来静默跳过父配置的校验和、范围检查、方向校验等安全约束。\n- 在合并逻辑中用\"有子配置就用子配置、否则用父配置\"的简单覆盖策略处理安全相关字段。\n\nWHY: SCOE adapter 的 `getCommandConfigs()` 合并卫星配置 + 基础配置时，卫星版同名 command 覆盖基础版。如果卫星 `send_frame` 没配 checksums，基础版带 checksum 的 `send_frame` 就被静默替换，校验和验证消失。任何\"子配置覆盖父配置\"的场景都有此风险。\n\n## 5. Boundary Exceptions"

> AGENT

三条已写入：

- **R17**（§4 Hard Rules）：expression condition 必须求值为 `boolean true`，禁止裸数字/裸变量
- **R18**（§4 Hard Rules）：配置分层合并不能静默丢失安全约束，子层省略必须显式继承
- **AND/OR 左结合**（§8 集成测试编写规范）：ConditionTerm 无优先级，混合时必须显式标注求值顺序
